1d6:["$","$L8a",null,{"as":"h2","id":"dark-mode","children":"Dark Mode"}]
1d7:["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"@osdk/react-components"}]," ships built-in dark-theme overrides that activate when ",["$","code",null,{"children":"[data-bp-color-scheme=\"dark\"]"}]," is set on an ancestor element (typically ",["$","code",null,{"children":"<html>"}],") — matching Blueprint's own convention so the OSDK + Blueprint tokens flip together. The supported way to set the attribute is ",["$","code",null,{"children":"<OsdkThemeProvider>"}]," from ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@osdk/react-components/experimental/theme"}],"; see ","$L262"," for the full reference, including ",["$","code",null,{"children":"defaultTheme=\"system\""}]," (the default, follows ",["$","code",null,{"children":"prefers-color-scheme"}],"), runtime toggles via ",["$","code",null,{"children":"useOsdkTheme"}],", and controlled mode for integrating with an external theme store."]}]
1d8:["$","p",null,{"children":["Under ",["$","code",null,{"children":"[data-bp-color-scheme=\"dark\"]"}]," Blueprint flips its own ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--bp-*"}]," tokens, so ",["$","code",null,{"children":"dark.css"}]," only re-maps the subset of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--osdk-*"}]," tokens that have no ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--bp-*"}]," counterpart or that intentionally diverge from Blueprint's value."]}]
